## Hierarchy in force (packet §G01, fleet law)

Fable principal → native Opus domain leads (O1–O8; `general-purpose` agent type + `model: opus` +
`ROUTE: ORCHESTRATION` + `WHY OPUS:`; the global guard admits by model family and those lines only;
the `mastermind-opus-*` agent types carry Read/Glob/Grep only and cannot reach the Fabric) → existing
Subagent Fabric task operators via the cluster lease broker (`pool plan/pick/run`; pools bailian,
minimax, grok, cursor, glm) → optional bounded workers. No native leaf swarm; no new control plane.
`mastermind-executive` MCP (authenticated Executive connector) is unauthenticated in this session
(OAuth consent = human-only gate); modifying Fabric admission through it is therefore unavailable here.

- FROZEN chart-return design (PP2-UI-b): `MobileSheet` renders children only while `open` (portal), so closing the ≤860px sheet (this includes the 820 tablet) unmounts `OptionsCompanion`. `MatrixCompanion.tsx:76` clears the pin on mount AND unmount. Today, "pin then look at the chart" on tablet/phone therefore loses the pin, the selection and the scroll. Design:
  - `OptionsRail` holds a return ticket {root, session, strike, expiry, perspective, viewScrollTop}.
  - "Show on chart" pins and hides the sheet without `close()`'s `onPin(null)`.
  - A fixed `options-chart-return` chip above the bottom nav reopens the sheet with `restore`.
  - The selection is restored only when root and session match. `.view` scrollTop is restored after layout.

- DECIDED the integration stays on this carrier, as a new serialized lane PP2-UI-c, frozen at `$S/leads/PP2UIC/commission.md`.
  - Ordering: it starts only after run 2 is accepted, and its base is run 2's accepted head. Run 2's packet is unchanged, and no file run 2 owns is touched concurrently.
  - Routing: normal Fabric (MiniMax on mb), the same worktree and branch as PP2-UI-a.
- DECIDED rulings (binding in the commission):
  1. A preset/exact range radio. Exact mode has four labelled text inputs, From, To, Min days and Max days, at 44px under touch.
  2. Prefill from the applied displayed scope.
  3. On every draft change, `parseScopeBounds` runs against the all-expiry declared or derived axis domain.
     - Invalid input disables Apply. It gives one EN/ZH sentence per issue code (all 12) with `aria-invalid`, and never a zero or empty fiction.
     - Valid input shows a preview of strike count, expiry count and day range.
  4. The applied exact scope lives in component state only: not in prefs, localStorage or any request.
     - It is re-qualified on every snapshot against that snapshot's source session, never `Date.now()`.
     - It feeds grid, scope grid, totals, structure and node context through a new pure `matrixForExactScope`, which preserves the axis basis.
     - A newer snapshot with nothing in range renders an explicit empty-scope state with the reason.
  5. Cancel/Escape/Apply transaction and focus semantics are unchanged.
  6. Selection is retained, with outside/back-in-scope behaviour. The pin is unchanged.
  7. Days 0–0 is the source-session expiry only. `no_expiries` blocks Apply rather than substituting the next expiry.
  8. `lib/optionsScopeBounds.ts` and `lib/optionsCompanion.ts` are byte-locked: the parent's PP2L-R1 PASS is bound to their exact bytes.
- FACT the commission carries:
  - RED tests (u1)–(u10) against a declared-grid fixture with an absent position, a measured zero and a pre-session expiry, under a mocked far-away wall clock;
  - five negative controls (Apply enabled while invalid; days from `Date.now`; bounds in prefs; always writing declared axes; no Escape restore);

The subtraction sits outside the `try`. Because the fix also removed the tide split's `try/except: pass`, such a value would now raise where it was swallowed before.

Unreadable, past, compact and tz-aware expiries are therefore dropped before `_accumulate_tide` (only caller: line 1013) and `_enrich_contract_row` (only caller: line 1073). The fix hardens the helper contract; it does not change today's live output.

P2 ledger DO_NOT_REDO, WS do_not_redo and preserved obligations outrank packet tasks; a conflicting OPT is re-scoped, never executed as written. P3 the seat-observed carrier table governs; packet states are snapshot testimony. P4 the packet adds scope and acceptance obligations, never owners, planes, queues, pollers or carriers. 2. Claim-vocabulary crosswalk ADOPTED (three orthogonal axes: class × availability × authority/lifecycle). observed / observed prints-OI / observed facts → OBSERVED (OI is an outstanding count, not opening intent; quote location is not dealer inventory). derived → DERIVED (deterministic transform under a named convention). modeled → DERIVED when assumptions are fixed and named (IV, Greeks, signed exposure), SCENARIO when conditional on a hypothetical state. modeled positioning / assumed-book → DERIVED with named assumptions, or SCENARIO when the assumption is varied; never OBSERVED dealer holdings. conditional / conditional scenarios → SCENARIO (a conditional hedge target is not a trade). hedge demand → DERIVED (local) or SCENARIO (between states); an executed hedge is never inferred. risk-neutral probability → DERIVED; a physical probability is FORECAST and requires OPT-091. predictive claims → FORECAST (family-specific qualification before any consumer authority). research / promoted / evaluated are NOT classes but authority or lifecycle states (a realized outcome is OBSERVED; a theoretical mark is DERIVED). missing / unknown / failed read / stale form the availability axis and are never coerced to zero, absent or stale (R04-B1 `read_failed` is not live).
